CHAP. X.
To erett a Figure the Catholick way of Morinus.


1. ID Ecauſce the rational way of Regiomontane will not
LJ hold univerſally through all the World (but from
66 Degrees of Latitude to the Pole, the whole Zodiack
vill preſs upon the Eaſt and Welt Angles,) Aforinas, that.
great Philoſopher and Aſtrologian (after ſome Confide-1
rations) fell upon a more Univerſal or Catholick way of
ſetting a Figure, by an unequal divifion of the Zodiack
by Circles of Latitude drawn through the Poles of the
Ecliprick, making with the Equator Oblique Angles, and
ſo by conſtitution and ſolution of aReftangle Spherical
angle, confiſting, firſt, of the Right or Oblique Aſcenſi-

CHAP, XI.
Of Eminent Fixed Stars neer the Ecliptick,

fixth, their Right Aſcenſion : the ſeventh, their Nature: |.
the eighth, their Magnitude: the ninth, the Equatiohs of
Declination : the tenth, the equations of their Right Aſ-
cenſions.
